Tamer detail view; End Turn button shown when it's the player's own turn in active combat; map view shows a "TURN ORDER" panel (top-left) matching the GM view. Mirrors `[id].vue`'s throw-aiming (`encounterMapRef`, `throwAimControllerId`, `throwAllyAimContext`, `handleThrowClick`/`cancelThrowAim`/`onThrowLandingSelected`, `handleThrowAllyClick`/`cancelThrowAllyAim`/`onThrowAllyLandingSelected`, `getAreaProtectTargetIds`, `executeClashAction` signature) and `handleIntercedeClaim(interceptorParticipantId, throwOptions?)` (Parts A/C); a `watch()` on the current intercede offer's `data` clears `playerIntercedeAreaChosenTarget`/cancels in-progress throw-aim if the chosen target drops out of `tamerAreaTargetIds`/`digimonAreaTargetIds`. Adds `hasThrowImpactRequest`/`currentThrowImpactRequest` computeds + `confirmThrowImpactAttack()` (Part B) — Throw Impact Attack modal prompts to roll a `basic-ranged` accuracy check (+`accuracyBonus` dice) against `targetIds` when the player's Clash Throw lands on a group of enemies, then resolves via the existing `attack` endpoint. Opens its own `useMapWebSocket` connection once `activeEncounter` is set, debounce-refetching on `encounter-updated`; poll interval lengthened `5000` → `30000` (Part E). Attack Result modal groups all targets of one AoE cast into a single popup via `attackResultGroup` (computed off a shared `groupId`/`areaGroupId`/`intercedeGroupId`) — see `showAttackResult`/`showAttackResultFromBattleLog`/`closeAttackResultModal`/`confirmAreaAttack`. `pendingAttacks` entries carry `targetParticipantId` (the per-target participant ID, set alongside the existing attacker `participantId`) so the dodge-response/battle-log watchers, `showAttackResult`'s `matchingLogEntry`, and `reconstructAttackResults`'s dedup guard all match per-target rather than grabbing the first result for a shared attacker. `attackResultGroupTotals` (set to `targets.length` in `confirmAreaAttack`) + `maybeShowGroupModal(groupId?)` gate `showAttackResultModal` so an AoE result popup only appears once every target's result is queued; modal template is `max-h-[85vh] overflow-y-auto` with compact per-target cards (`p-3 mb-2`, merged "HIT! vs {target}" header) to fit 4+ targets. **Inspiration spending (2026-06-14)**: `confirmAttack`/`confirmAreaAttack` apply an optional pre-roll Act of Inspiration +5 to `accuracyPool` (toggle on `AttackRiderOptions`), then open a new **Accuracy Roll Review modal** (`showAccuracyReview`/`accuracyReview`/`accuracyReviewContext`, Teleported `z-50`) hosting `RollInterceptPanel` (re-roll / +1 die) before `confirmAccuracyReview()` calls `performAttack`; the modal has no cancel option — once the accuracy roll happens the attack must be confirmed. The Dodge and Counterattack modals each gained their own pre-roll Act of Inspiration toggle (`dodgeActOfInspirationEnabled`, `counterattackActOfInspirationEnabled`, always +5 dice) feeding async `rollDodgeDice()`/`rollCounterattackDice()`, plus `RollInterceptPanel` on the rolled result. `InspirationPanel` (extracted from the former inline block) is used in card view and as a new floating Map Mode panel (bottom-right, `z-50`), making Inspiration spend reachable in Map Mode. **Roll history capture (2026-07-08)**: `performAttributeRoll()` (~line 1898) and `performTormentRoll()` (~line 1927, torment label `Torment: {selectedTorment.name}`, `passed: total >= 12`) each fire-and-forget POST their result to `/api/campaigns/[campaignId]/rolls` via a shared `logRoll()` helper (~line 1913), feeding the global `RollLogPanel.vue`. Failure is swallowed (`.catch(() => {})`) so a roll-log outage never blocks the roll modal. **(2026-07-29)** `loadData()` now fetches with `{visibleToPlayers: true}`, so GM-staged encounters never reach the client. It then builds a candidate list of **every** visible encounter this tamer is in (rather than `find()`ing the first active one) and picks by priority: has a pending request for me → `phase === 'combat'` → most recently updated — fixing the case where a tamer queued into two encounters only ever saw one. `hasUnrespondedInitiativeRequest` returns `false` while `hasUnrespondedDigimonRequest` is true (both modals are `Teleport`ed `fixed inset-0 z-50`, so the later-declared initiative one would otherwise paint over a re-sent digimon picker), and `selectedDigimonId` resets whenever the picker (re)opens so a re-sent prompt starts blank. **(2026-07-29)** Reserve-aware: `myParticipants` excludes `inReserve` (a staged character can't act, doesn't count for `isMyTurn`, stays out of the HUD); `myReservedParticipants` drives a greyed "⏸ Waiting to join" strip; `visibleParticipants` (drops reserves that aren't mine) backs `getEnemyTargets`, `getClashTargets`, `getPlayerDirectTargets`, the Enemy Scan picker and the Intercede Options list. `playerPlacementMode` extends to "setup/initiative **or** I have a live participant with no position", so a character just deployed from reserve can be placed mid-combat. **(2026-11-03d)** Dodge modal: new `dodgeBlockedByClash` computed (request `cannotDodge` flag OR live `isAttackedFromOutsideClash(attacker, target)`) drives the 0d6 pool, the "locked in a clash — cannot dodge" banner, and hides/skips the Act of Inspiration dodge bonus so no inspiration is spent on a dodge that can't succeed. **(2026-11-03e)** The sidebar Quick Reference card is static template text reflecting the dice-pool combat rules (Accuracy d6 vs Dodge d6, 5–6 = success; 2 Simple Actions per turn, Complex = both); update it whenever action costs or the hit/damage formula change. **(2026-11-03g)** Intercede Options also sits left of ✕ Close Map in map view (`openIntercedeOptions()`, shared with the card-view button); the list comes from `encounter.intercedeOptOuts` via `myIntercedeOptOuts` (`~/utils/intercedeOptOuts`), the save sends `{ ownerId: tamer.id }` and no longer loops `intercede-skip` (the server prunes queued prompts), the popup's area-attack Never sends only the chosen target (`optOutTargetIds`), and a prompt whose targets are all opted out is auto-skipped. **(2026-11-03h)** Willpower Check modal (digivolution): Cancel is shown only before the roll. On a failure its slot becomes "✦ Spend Inspiration" (`rerollWillpowerWithInspiration()` → `spendInspiration(…, 'reroll', 1)`, re-rolls via `rollWillpower()` only if the spend succeeded, then `resyncCombatState('digivolve')`), rendered only while `currentCombatInspiration >= 1`; it shares `inspirationSpendLoading`, which also disables the primary button. The `encounter-state` handler compares `evolutionFormsKey()` (`~/utils/evolutionForms`, now used by `utils/combatRecords.ts`) before/after and refetches evolution lines when a participant changed form, so a GM force-evolve/devolve from the Manage modal updates the Digivolve/Devolve buttons (card view and map popup) live. **(2026-11-07b) Targeted updates:** `applyEncounterUpdate(incoming, 'ws' \| 'action' \| 'fetch', {version, action})` is the one way an encounter replaces page state — gate (stale snapshots dropped, `currentEncounter` restored), `needsFullReload` hand-off, then `activeEncounter`/`currentEncounter`/`myRequests`/`reconstructAttackResults`, then `refreshAffectedRecords(planRecordRefresh(...))` (only the touched Digimon via `/api/digimon?ids=`, the Tamer, evolution lines, partner list). `applyActionResult(result, action)` is what every combat action handler (movement, attack, dodge, counterattack, intercede claim/skip/opt-out, Divine Protection, Inspiration, orders, digivolve/devolve, End Turn…) calls with its response; with no response it falls back to `resyncCombatState()` (one gated encounter GET), never `loadData()`. `useDigivolveWillpower`/`useClashAttack` refresh callbacks and `encounterWs.onReconnect` use `resyncCombatState()`. New log entries come from `newBattleLogEntries(battleLogTotal(before), next)`. `loadData()` remains for first mount, the Refresh button, the 120 s poll, the `encounter-updated` fallback (debounced 200 ms), digimon import and encounters that left the dashboard. **(2026-11-07c) Pass until my next turn:** `useIntercedePass({ encounter, ownerId, send, apply, onError })` (adapters → `$fetch` `intercede-pass` and the gated `applyActionResult(result, 'intercede-pass')`) supplies `isPassingOnIntercede`/`intercedePassBusy`/`startIntercedePass`/`resumeIntercedePass`. A **Pass until my next turn** button sits under Skip (main prompt), Skip All (area prompt) and Skip All (map-mode area picker); while passing, a Quick-Reaction prompt that still needs an answer says so. **(2026-11-07d) Area defenses:** `pendingAreaDefenseGroup` (the first cast with two or more of my unanswered dodge requests) opens `areaDefenseBatch` via `syncAreaDefenseBatch()` (a watcher on requests/responses/participants also reconciles an open panel with only that cast's requests); `areaDefenseRequestIds` keeps those requests off `currentDodgeRequest`, so the single modal is unchanged for single-target attacks and counterattacks. `submitAreaDefense` sends one target's roll as held and reports failure instead of throwing; `areaDefenseDismissal` (`useAutoDismiss`, keyed only when the panel is settled) runs `cleanupAreaDefense` — `cleanupDodgeExchange` per request/response, then clears the panel. **Pass until my next turn:** The intercede-offer watcher calls `isOfferSuppressed` (permanent opt-out **or** pass) and `autoSkipSuppressedOffer(requestId)` (quiet: an in-flight set stops duplicates, failures are only logged); a second watcher on `isPassingOnIntercede` answers a prompt already on screen when a pass starts elsewhere. |

### Layouts

| Layout | File | Usage |
|---|---|---|
| `default` | `app/layouts/default.vue` | All DM/GM pages; header nav + footer. **(2026-07-12)** Header renders `<AccountWidget variant="inline" />` as a sibling right after the logo `NuxtLink`, before the nav |
| `player` | `app/layouts/player.vue` | Player-facing views; simplified nav |

**Global (layout-independent) mount:** `app/app.vue` renders `<RollLogPanel />` unconditionally as a sibling of `<NuxtLayout>` (present on every route). **(2026-07-12)** `<AccountWidget />` there is now conditional: `v-if="route.meta.layout === 'player' || route.meta.layout === false"`, so it only renders as the floating fallback on the header-less `player` layout and `layout: false` pages (e.g. the map editor); `default`-layout pages instead get an inline copy rendered directly in `layouts/default.vue`'s header — see Shared Components below.

### Shared Components

**2026-11-08i AoE voxel preview:** `MapCanvas.vue`'s `updateAoeHighlight` now delegates to `renderAoeVolume`, which deduplicates hit/blocked cells and calls the existing `buildVoxelSurface` over their combined area. It builds at most two exposed-surface meshes (orange hit/red blocked) with opacity 0.3 and depth writing disabled, plus one outer-crease outline. A cell is a full unit-height voxel above the floor rather than a thin tile slab. There are no internal cube faces or flat grid edges, including at hit/blocked interfaces. Only hit cells remain in `areaHighlightCells` for targeting. `clearAoeHighlights` disposes mesh/outline geometry and materials on replacement, clear and unmount.

**2026-11-08h occupied squares and map interaction:** The size exceptions apply in both directions: either unit being Tiny permits sharing; otherwise either unit being Small permits passage only; otherwise the units block each other, regardless of faction. Tamers are medium for this policy. `EncounterMap.vue` supplies type-correct sizes, and `useMapMovement.ts` checks the mover's whole body for both BFS and A* and rejects occupied non-landable path endpoints. `MapCanvas.vue` applies the same policy to charge/Pass, placement and GM Move destinations.

`MapCanvas.vue` opens an initial `MapRadialMenu` labeled **Choose character** whenever a clicked token footprint or ground cell contains multiple characters. The live candidates then use `selectMapParticipant` for valid attack/intercede targeting, GM actions, owned actions or current health. Removed/moved choices cannot dispatch stale selections; ambiguous stacks do not start arbitrary token drags. GM Move now has its own aim height, shares normal Move's plane/ghost logic, refreshes the ghost immediately on scroll and follows the plane with the camera so it remains visible above the original camera height. It permits any distance and open-air/water/earth destinations without movement qualities, while retaining occupied-square and solid-voxel landing checks. It still emits `gm-moved` for a free position update, rather than a paid combat move.

**2026-11-08g partner path visibility:** `MapCanvas.vue` was receiving complete flying/digging paths whose intermediate cells unnecessarily dipped below the floor; ground geometry occluded those yellow markers while endpoints and distance remained visible. `utils/pathfinding.ts` now selects fewer elevation changes among equally short routes in both `searchReachable` and `findPath`, keeping flat-ground previews visible without changing the marker renderer or movement capabilities.

**2026-11-08f movement invalidation cost:** `EncounterMap.vue`'s `mapSignature = computed(() => JSON.stringify(map.value))` depends only on the map and its nested reactive values. The existing synchronous value-key watcher reads `mapSignature.value` alongside the unchanged movement inputs; token moves and polling updates no longer traverse/serialize map proxies. In-place voxel/wall edits, door toggles and map replacement refresh the signature before key comparison. The outer key still serializes the cached string, so position-only cost is reduced rather than independent of serialized map size. The supplied `bench-map-watch.cjs` now compares old-deep, new-stringify and computed-signature variants at three synthetic sizes; these are benchmark samples, not map limits. Other deep watchers and serialization in `EncounterMap.vue`/`MapCanvas.vue` are unchanged.

**2026-11-08e movement previews:** `EncounterMap.vue` preserves hovered paths across equivalent polling snapshots and health/action-only updates, while changes to movement inputs invalidate the cached search synchronously. Post-charge hover routing uses `chargeMoveParticipantId` when no ordinary mover is active. `MapCanvas.vue` renders path markers, destination ghosts and labels at the mover's footprint center without changing committed anchor coordinates; marker caching includes mover identity and dimensions.

| `MapCanvas` | `components/MapCanvas.vue` | **(2026-11-07j)** Every way of putting a character on the map shares one rule: `evaluatePlacementAt` gathers the inputs and `evaluatePlacement` (`utils/placementRules.ts`) decides — click placement, GM free-move, token drag and HUD-card drop. For players **every cell of the unit's real width × depth footprint** must be a spawn cell (`makeSpawnCellChecker` over the map's flagged tiles/voxels, 2026-11-07k), so a large unit can't overhang the placeable area; solid voxels and other units block everyone, GM included. New props `draggableParticipantIds` and `externalDragParticipantId`, new emit `unit-dragged(participantId, cell)`. **Token drag:** a capture-phase `pointerdown` on the canvas (it must run before OrbitControls, whose left button is PAN) feeds `createTokenDragController` (`utils/tokenDrag.ts`), which locks the camera for the press, treats under 4px as a click, previews a green/red footprint ghost through `hoverGhostGroup`, commits via `unit-dragged`, swallows the `click` that follows, and always hands the camera back (drop, refused drop, Escape, `pointercancel`, unmount). It stays off while another mode owns the left button (GM map-edit tools, move/charge/attack aiming, click placement, GM free-move, target picking). **HUD drop:** native `dragover`/`drop` on the container element (so a label or overlay inside the map still counts); `dragover` only calls `preventDefault()` over a valid cell, so refused cells show the not-allowed cursor. **(2026-11-03f)** Single-target **ranged** reticules require line of sight: `updateReticules()` drops a target unless `canSeeFootprint(losIndex, …)` (`utils/lineOfSight.ts`) finds a clear line from the attacker's footprint centre to the target's centre or any of its cells. `losIndex` is rebuilt at the end of `buildMap()` from map data (not meshes, so the Y-clip and ghost walls never change targeting), and `hasLineOfSight`/`clipToLos` wrap `losClear`/`losClip`, so area shapes share the same blockers. Reticules also refresh on any position change. Mesh dimensions come from `MAP_GEOMETRY`. Door panels register in `doorMeshes`; open doors render swung on their hinge into the wall's own cell. The NPC and player radial menus group `Open Door`/`Close Door` choices in a nested Doors ring for doors adjacent to the radial's unit (`doorsAdjacentTo`); hover tints the door (`highlightDoor`), click emits `door-toggle(doorId, isOpen)`. **(2026-08-19)** Requires an `attackerCaps: MovementCapabilities` prop (supplied by `EncounterMap.vue`'s `attackerCaps` computed, which reuses `npcMoveCaps()`); it replaced a local `getAttackerCapabilities()` that zeroed `jumpRange`/`jumpHeight` and could not be fixed in place because the `digimonMap` prop carries no `baseStats`. Used by `isPassLandingValid`/`anyPassLandingValid` (both now also take `attackerPos`, forwarded to `canLandOn` as its `origin`) so a `Jumper` can finish a `[Pass]` in mid-air within jump height. — Three.js 3D isometric renderer; handles billboards, tiles, walls, movement highlights, health overlays, reticules, AOE area highlights (amber tiles in `aoeGroup`); emits `npc-action` (GM NPC radial: Move/Stance/Attack), `player-action` (player radial: tamer gets Move/Direct/Bolster Direct/Orders/Stance, digimon gets Attack/Stance/Digivolve), `target-selected` (single-target click), `area-attack-confirmed(targetParticipantIds: string[], areaShapeData: AreaShapeData \| null)` (AOE click — covered participant IDs plus the `AreaShapeData` used to compute them, from `~/utils/areaShapes`, so the AoE can be recomputed later e.g. for Intercede "Throw Ally Out of the Blast"), `charge-target-selected(attackerId, destination, targetId | null)` (Charge Attack move step). **Throw aiming** (Clash Throw, Part A/Intercede "Throw Ally Out of the Blast", Part C): `defineExpose({ startThrowAim, cancelThrowAim, startThrowAllyAim, cancelThrowAllyAim })` lets parent pages enter/exit a landing-cell-picking mode that highlights `getReachableCells(originPos, maxDistance, PROJECTILE_CAPS, map)` cells (filtered by footprint validity and, for the ally variant, `excludeCells` = the AoE's cells); on click, emits `throw-landing-selected(controllerId, thrownTargetId, landingPos)` or `throw-ally-landing-selected(interceptorParticipantId, allyParticipantId, landingPos)`. When `selectedAttack` prop set: shows reticules on valid enemies, renders dynamic AOE preview on mouse move, confirms on left-click / cancels on right-click. **All area shapes share one Blast-style 3D aimer** (`isAreaTargeting`/`usesVerticalAim`): full-cell ghost volume incl. empty air, scroll-wheel vertical aim (`adjustAimY` re-pitches via `lastAreaAimEvent`), LoS dimming + Range/Effective-Limit rings. **`[Pass]` two-step aim**: `passStep: 'movement'|'extra'|null` — step 1 picks direction + movement length (clamped to the attacker's Movement stat) with live AoE preview; clicking locks `passLockedDir`/`passMovementLength`, and (if RAM > 0) enters step 2, which shows a static blue extra-movement range band (`passRangeGroup`, 0..RAM along the locked direction, same styling as `movementHighlightGroup`) while the orange AoE preview extends live within it; a second click confirms with `{movement, ram: extra}` (RAM = 0 confirms immediately after step 1). Perpendicular mouse movement and scroll-wheel pitch are ignored during step 2 (`adjustAimY` no-ops); state resets via `resetPassState()`. AOE hit-detection and origin geometry are fully 3D, sharing `FootprintDims`/`getFootprintDimensions`/`getFootprintCells` from `~/utils/movementRules`: burst's sphere origins enumerate the attacker's **whole 3D body** (every floor of its footprint, not just the base), and cone/close-blast/line/pass originate from the body's leading edge **vertically centred** on its full height (`leadingEdgeOrigin`); target membership tests the **full 3D footprint box** (`footprintIntersectsArea`/`getParticipantFootprintDims`). **Charge Attack** (`chargeMode: 'before'|'after'|null`, `chargeMoveParticipantId: string|null` props): when `chargeMode === 'before'`, melee reticules and click-targeting (`tryChargeMove`, via shared `meleeInRange` helper, now 3D-box-aware) also test footprint-in-range from every `reachableCells` anchor, not just the attacker's current position, picking the anchor closest to the attacker's current position; for area-shaped charge attacks, clicking a `reachableCells` cell instead emits `charge-target-selected(attackerId, cell, null)`. `chargeMoveParticipantId` reuses the NPC-move UI (mirrors `npcMoveParticipantId` watcher) for the post-attack "Move After" relocation step. **Active-turn highlight**: the orange outline (`buildSprites()`) is shown for `p.id === activeParticipantId` **or** `p.id === secondaryActiveParticipantId` (the active participant's tamer/partner-digimon counterpart), so both members of a player pair are highlighted together; `digimonMap` entries carry `partnerId?: string | null`. **Stance border**: each `.char-token` overlay's border color reflects `p.currentStance` via `STANCE_COLORS` (from `app/utils/stanceModifiers.ts`), independent of the active-turn outline. **Low-wound overlay**: `updateCharacterOverlays()` computes `woundFilter: 'yellow' | 'red' | null` from remaining wounds (`1 - currentWounds/maxWounds`, same convention as the health-bar popup), reading `currentWounds`/`maxWounds` off the participant itself first (falls back to `info.currentWounds`/`info.woundBoxes` from `tamerMap`/`digimonMap`, since duplicate NPC participants share one `entityId`-keyed `info`) — a `.char-token-wound-filter` div renders a 20%-opacity yellow wash at ≤50% remaining or red at ≤25% (red takes priority). **Depth-sorted tokens**: `updateCharacterOverlays()` computes `distToChar` (camera-to-character distance, also used for occlusion) unconditionally and sets `zIndex: Math.round(1_000_000 - distToChar * 1000)` on each overlay; the `.char-token`'s inline `:style` applies it, so the character closer to the camera always paints above one further away, regardless of turn/placement order. **Occlusion respects the Y-clip (2026-07-28)**: the camera→token raycast's occluder list (`wallMeshes` + `voxelMeshes`) is filtered to `m.visible` before casting, because Three.js raycasting ignores `Object3D.visible` — geometry clipped away by the `clipY` control no longer hides tokens behind it. Game-rules LoS (`hasLos`/`clipToLos`) is unaffected and still sees clipped geometry. **Camera (2026-07-28)**: `OrbitControls` with polar locked at π/3 and LEFT=pan / MIDDLE=dolly / RIGHT=custom. Azimuth is **free, not snapped** — right-drag rotates continuously (`applyRightDragRotation` → `setAzimuth`, 2π per viewport width), Q/E ease to the next 45° multiple over 220ms via `stepAzimuth`/`azimuthTween`/`tickAzimuthTween` (also serving as a re-align from any angle). **Target outline colors (2026-11-03)**: targetable participants are marked by their volume-outline box (`buildSprites()`'s `LineSegments`), not an HTML overlay. The old red `.target-reticule` circle inside `.char-token` was removed, so "reticule" in older entries now means this red box. Every id in `reticuleParticipantIds` (any reticule flow: single-target attacks, clash-initiate, intercede/Direct via `selectableParticipantIds`, and participants inside an area attack's highlighted cells) gets a red `0xff2222` outline, and the one a click would select turns green `0x33ff66` while hovered. `reticuleTargetFromHits(hits)` holds the target-pick lookup (reticuled sprite-hitbox hit first, else a reticuled participant whose footprint covers the hit ground/voxel tile) and is shared by `onCanvasClick` and `updateTargetHover(event)` (called in `onMouseMove` right after the right-drag early return), so the green token is always the click target. `updateTargetHover` skips the raycast and clears the hover when no reticules exist or a click would not reach target selection (movement mode, area aiming, map-edit tools, placement/GM move). Outline colors come from `participantBorderColor(id)` (hovered → green, targetable → red, active/secondary-active → orange `0xff8800`, else `0x334466`). `buildSprites()` registers each outline material in `borderMaterials` (participantId → `LineBasicMaterial`) so `setHoveredTarget(id)` recolors only the previous and new outlines via `color.setHex`, and `refreshBorderColors()` recolors all of them. A `watch(reticuleParticipantIds)` calls `refreshBorderColors()` whenever the targetable set changes (also dropping the hover once its id is no longer targetable); the hover also clears on canvas `mouseleave`. Outlines draw with `depthTest: false`, so a targetable participant behind a wall still shows its red box. | Many props including `selectedAttack: {tags, range, type?, bit, movement?, ram?, sizeAboveLarge?, attackerParticipantId?}`, `chargeMode`, `chargeMoveParticipantId`, `secondaryActiveParticipantId` |
