**Transparent voxel volumes (2026-11-06, refined 2026-11-06b):** `MapCanvas.vue` calls `buildVoxelSurface(cells, groupForCell)` from `utils/voxelSurface.ts` to render the exposed faces and crease edges of visible voxels with effective opacity below 1. The grouping key combines material id with effective elemental affinity (`voxel.element`, falling back to material id). Adjacent matching cells form a single outlined volume: coplanar seams and shared faces within that group are omitted. Different element/material groups retain their interface faces and boundary edges, with coincident outline segments drawn once. Color overrides alone do not split a group. Separate meshes retain each cell's voxel metadata for editing and selection. Changing `clipY` rebuilds the groups from the remaining visible cells, restoring the exposed top faces and outline. `clearTransparentVoxelOutline()` disposes the previous line geometry/material on rebuild and unmount. This affects rendering only; movement and game-rule line of sight still use the map data.

**Shared combat workflows (2026-11-06c).** `encounters/[id].vue` and `player/[tamerId].vue` mount the same Willpower and Clash Attack dialogs. Their composables keep state per mounted page and accept typed adapters for encounter access, attack stats, map actions and refresh behavior. `utils/attackAccuracy.ts` prepares a captured rider selection and one dice result for either a single-target or area attack; the player still spends pre-roll Inspiration and opens mandatory Accuracy Roll Review, while the GM still submits directly and logs the original/final Huge Power dice. Clash initiation/checks, pin/end/throw actions, area result tracking and all server combat rules remain in their existing locations.

### State Management

No Pinia or Vuex. All reactive state lives in **composables** (Vue 3 `ref`/`computed`). Campaign admission persists through signed httpOnly credentials; boolean cookies are display hints only; account login state persists via `useAuth()`'s module-level `user` ref, restored from the `httpOnly` `session` cookie on app init — see Cross-Cutting Concerns → Auth Flow.

**Composables — `app/composables/`:**

| Composable | File | Responsibility |
|---|---|---|
| `useCampaigns` | `useCampaigns.ts` | List, fetch, create, delete campaigns; password verification |
| `useAuth` | `useAuth.ts` | **(2026-07-12)** Module-level (singleton-like) `user`/`loading`/`error`/`initialized` refs; `register`/`login`/`logout`/`fetchMe`/`searchUsers`. Backs `AccountWidget.vue` and `auth/login.vue` |
| `useCampaignContext` | `useCampaignContext.ts` | Extract `campaignId` from route; fetch current campaign |
| `useTamers` | `useTamers.ts` | CRUD for tamers; `fetchTamers(campaignId)` |
| `useTamerForm` | `useTamerForm.ts` | Reactive form state for tamer create/edit; derived stats |
| `useTamerValidation` | `useTamerValidation.ts` | Validate tamer data before save; torment rules, skill limits |
| `useDigimon` | `useDigimon.ts` | CRUD for digimon; filtering by campaign/partner |
| `useDigimonForm` | `useDigimonForm.ts` | Reactive form state for digimon create/edit |
| `useDigimonStats` | `useDigimonStats.ts` | Compute derived stats (brains, body, agility, woundBoxes, BIT/CPU/RAM, baseMovement, baseBit/Cpu/Ram for System Boost cap, currentSpeedyMaxRanks) |
| `useDigimonDP` | `useDigimonDP.ts` | DP budget tracking; validate DP allocation |
| `useDigimonAttacks` | `useDigimonAttacks.ts` | Add/edit/remove attacks on digimon form |
| `useDigimonQualities` | `useDigimonQualities.ts` | Add/remove qualities; DP cost calculation; `bossQualityDPCap` (stage-scaled cap on `category: 'boss'` quality spend for Dark Digivolutions, `Infinity` for `isEnemy`) enforced in `handleAddQuality` |
| `useDigimonValidation` | `useDigimonValidation.ts` | Validate digimon data; stat range enforcement |
| `useEvolution` | `useEvolution.ts` | Evolution line CRUD; advance/devolve stage |
| `useDigivolveWillpower` | `useDigivolveWillpower.ts` | Per-page pending digivolution, 3d6 + Willpower/XP, campaign DC, warp selection and success/failure requests. Typed callbacks supply the rolling tamer participant, Inspiration spend, page refresh and player-only reroll refresh; the player passes its shared Inspiration loading ref. Cancelling is allowed only before rolling, and busy guards prevent duplicate rerolls/submissions. |
| `useClashAttack` | `useClashAttack.ts` | Per-page controller melee attack picker, Charge stay/move choice and area-clash context. Injected page adapters supply typed participants/attacks, submission, refresh, area targeting and the follow-up map move. Uses the live `CombatParticipant` from `useEncounters`, avoiding the legacy complex-action shape in `types/index.ts`. |
| `useAreaDefenseBatch` | `useAreaDefenseBatch.ts` | **(2026-11-07d)** One defense panel's state for an area cast. Adapters: `defense`, `currentInspiration`, `actOfInspirationCost`, `spend` (resolves true only when the server accepted it), `submit` (returns `{ ok, responseId?, summary?, message? }`), `isAnswered`, `isTargetPresent`, `rollDie?`. API: `open`, `close`, `reconcile`, `ownsRequest`, `toggleAct`, `rollOne`, `rerollOne`, `addDieOne`, `rollAllUnrolled`, `submitOne`, `submitReady`, `cleanupTargets`, plus `views`, `entries`, `active`, `isSettled`, `running`. No free re-rolls, paid-once Act of Inspiration, serialized spends, resend-the-same-roll retries |
| `useIntercedePass` | `useIntercedePass.ts` | **(2026-11-07c)** "Pass until my next turn" for the player page. Adapter-injected (`encounter`, `ownerId`, `send`, `apply`, `onError`). `isPassing`/`myPass` are computed from the synced encounter (`getActiveTemporaryPass`), so other tabs, reconnect resyncs and the expiry at the Tamer's next turn need no local state; `start()`/`resume()` post `intercede-pass`, hand the returned encounter to `apply`, and ignore a second click while `busy` |
| `useAutoDismiss` | `useAutoDismiss.ts` | **(2026-11-07, fixed 2026-11-07e)** `useAutoDismiss({ key, cleanup, delayMs = 5000 })` for completed combat results. The timer starts when `key` becomes non-null, restarts on a new key, and is cancelled by a null key, `dismiss()` or scope disposal. Cleanup is deduplicated per result key, so a replacement result can expire while an earlier result's cleanup awaits. The player page uses one per result dialog; decisions and incomplete result groups pass no key. |
| `useEncounters` | `useEncounters.ts` | Encounter CRUD; all combat action API calls. **(2026-07-29)** `fetchEncounters(campaignId?, opts?: {visibleToPlayers?})`; `duplicateEncounter(id, {name?, mode?})`. **`rebuildTurnOrder(participants, digimonMap?, activeParticipantId?) → {turnOrder, currentTurnIndex}`** is the single source of truth for turn order — sorts by initiative desc, drops the `gm` metadata row and tamer-grouped partner digimon, and returns the index the *active* participant landed at, so a mid-combat insertion/removal can't silently hand the turn to someone else. Used by `addParticipant`, `removeParticipant` and (on the GM page) `handleSaveInitiative` + both `processResponse` branches. Its helper `isGroupedUnderTamer(participant, participants, digimonMap)` hides a partner digimon **only when its partner tamer is also a participant** — the previous `!d?.partnerId` test dropped a solo-added partner digimon from `turnOrder` entirely, so it was invisible and never got a turn. `removeParticipant` keeps its careful active-participant/round-wrap math, then rebuilds (a removed tamer un-groups their partner, who must claim their own slot). `createParticipant` ids are `${type}-${entityId}-${Date.now()}-${rand}` — the random suffix prevents same-millisecond collisions in the add-quantity loop, which would make two participants share one `participantPositions` entry |
| `useMap` | `useMap.ts` | Map CRUD; `fetchMaps`, `fetchMap`, `createMap`, `updateMap`, `deleteMap` |
| `useMapWebSocket` | `useMapWebSocket.ts` | WebSocket client with auto-reconnect (5 retries exp backoff); `send()` + `onMessage()`. **(2026-07-08)** Second param `basePath: 'encounters' \| 'campaigns' = 'encounters'` selects the URL template (`/api/{basePath}/{id}/ws`); default preserves every existing encounter-room caller unchanged. Used with `'campaigns'` by `RollLogPanel.vue` to join the campaign-wide `campaign:{id}` roll-log room. **(2026-11-07b)** `onReconnect(handler)` fires when the socket opens again after an earlier open (pushes sent during the gap are lost); not on the first open, and `hasOpened` resets when the encounter id changes. Both encounter pages use it to resync |
| `useMapMovement` | `useMapMovement.ts` | BFS reachable cells + A* pathfinding; `computeReachable`, `computePath`, `detectCapabilities`; exports `PROJECTILE_CAPS: MovementCapabilities` (`{ canFly: true, canJump: true, jumpHeight: 99, jumpRange: 99, canClimb: false, canSwim: true, canDig: false }`) — used by `MapCanvas.vue`'s throw-aiming reachable-cell highlighting (Clash Throw / "Throw Ally Out of the Blast") |
| `useMapEditor` | `useMapEditor.ts` | Atomic indexed edits publish one root/revision and return whether anything changed; no-op strokes skip history and saving. Fifty delta history commands retain before/after rows and ordering. `setDoorsOpen`/`updateStructure` share that commit path; external map replacement invalidates stale history. Ramp direction/material metadata survives painting and undo/redo; `applyVoxelDraw(start,end,shape='block')` clears direction on blocks. `applyDoor()` toggles an existing door. |
| `useMapAutosave` | `useMapAutosave.ts` | Per-map ordered save queues; 300ms debounce/1500ms max wait, immutable changed-collection refs and one in-flight request per map. `enqueue`, `flush`, `retry`, `dispose`, `status`, `error`, `hasUnsaved`; compact save responses never replace newer local roots. Notifications run after acknowledgement. |
| `useMapRotation` | `useMapRotation.ts` | Per-encounter Y-rotation stored in localStorage |
| `useAttackTags` | `useAttackTags.ts` | Parse attack tag strings; resolve tag effects. **(2026-07-28)** Third optional param `eddySoulRules` (threaded from `useDigimonForm` → `useDigimonAttacks`): when `chromeWeaponNoWeaponRankRequired` is on and the Digimon owns Digizoid Weaponry (Chrome) but no `weapon` quality, offers a one-use `Chrome Digizoid` attack tag so the bonus can be designated to a single attack; not offered if the Digimon has Weapon Rank (Chrome then applies to all `[Weapon]`-tagged attacks as normal) |
| `useBaseStatRanges` | `useBaseStatRanges.ts` | Min/max base stat lookup per stage |
| `useLibraryImportExport` | `useLibraryImportExport.ts` | Legacy export/import of campaign library (tamers, digimon, maps) as plain JSON arrays — no rules metadata, drops partner/evolution links. Still used for maps and as the legacy-array import path (DM library only, behind an explicit confirm); per-card tamer/digimon Export buttons now use `useCharacterBundle` instead |
| `useCharacterBundle` | `useCharacterBundle.ts` | **(2026-07-11)** v2 `dda-character-bundle` export/import: `buildCharacterBundle`/`exportCharacterBundle(tamer, rules?)` (tamer + partner digimon + evolution lines + chain-referenced digimon, `localKey`-linked), `exportDigimonBundle(digimon, rules?)`, `parseBundleFile(file)` (classifies bundle vs legacy array), `importBundle(bundle, {campaignId \| sandbox, partnerTamerId?})` — client-orchestrated: tamer → digimon without links (collect localKey→newId) → evolutionPathIds pass → evolvesFromId pass (two passes so the PUT's bidirectional sync can't let a sibling's path update clobber a child's true pre-evolution) → evolution lines with remapped chain `digimonId`s. Rule-parity checks (`diffCreationRules`) happen in the calling pages BEFORE `importBundle` runs (warn + block) |
| `useCreationRulesContext` | `useCreationRulesContext.ts` | **(2026-07-11)** Provide/inject rules source for `TamerFormPage`/`DigimonFormPage`: `provideCreationRules(ref<CreationRules>)` (workshop pages) / `useCreationRulesContext()` — returns the same shape `useCampaignContext()` exposes for rules (campaignLevel, campaignRules, skillRenames, eddySoulRules, houseRules, skillOrdersEnabled) plus `creationRules` (snapshot for save payloads; null on campaign pages) and a no-op `loadCampaign` in workshop mode. Falls back to `useCampaignContext()` when nothing is provided; the provided context must always win on /workshop routes (the global `current-campaign` useState can hold a stale campaign) |

---

## 6. Dependency Graph

**ORM upgrade (2026-11-10):** `drizzle-orm` is pinned to 0.45.4. PostgreSQL schema, update-returning, transaction scopes and row locks retain their interfaces; no schema migration. Nuxt/Vue and Drizzle Kit are a later verification group.

**Hardening dependencies (2026-11-10):** Nuxt -> nuxt-security 2.7.0 -> SSR nonce/header plugins; security-csp -> runtime allowed origins; Vue image bindings -> imageFallback + client error capture. These require Node 24. Container packaging includes only production output.

**Security command graph (2026-11-10):** HTTP/WS -> campaignSecurity -> stored sessions/grants/credentials; entitySecurity -> runtime validation + linked scope. Encounter handlers -> encounterCommand/attackCommand -> serialized queue + databaseScope -> encounterMovement/encounterUpdate/encounterTurn/attackMovement + trusted attack/digivolution services -> persisted state -> deferred RoomState/broadcast. Clients submit typed intents; server notifications refresh snapshots. No database migration.

**2026-11-09k local lighting:** `mapVoxels.ts` declares optional `MapVoxelLightSourceDefinition` metadata and `getVoxelLightSource`, honoring custom colors and excluding carved windows. `mapTerrainChunks.ts` owns `createMapVoxelLights` from `utils/mapVoxelLights.ts`, updates matching source-instance colors and synchronizes lights only when chunks change. `MapCanvas` calls the terrain manager's `updateAnimation(timeSeconds)`; no package, endpoint or stored schema changes.

**2026-11-09j mobility:** `useDigimon`, the player movement display and both map prop builders supply Basic/full Movement and CPU/RAM; client `useMapMovement` and server `mapMovement` share weighted searches in `pathfinding` and water/jump/fall helpers in `movementRules`. `combatSpatial` supplies derived support-action and fall profiles; `endOfTurnGravity` distinguishes innate swimming from Extra Swimmer buoyancy. The optional step-cost callback preserves uniform-distance projectile and forced-displacement searches.

**2026-11-09f map performance:** `utils/mapEdits.ts` stores revisions and affected collection/cell metadata in WeakMaps keyed by raw map roots, with numeric previous-revision links (no retained root chain). `useMapEditor`, `EncounterMap` and `mapTerrainChunks` share that metadata. `utils/mapTerrainChunks.ts` uses Three.js instanced opaque meshes and one per-cell sorted BatchedMesh for transparent cells, with material/color/opacity/ramp compatibility and a one-cell boundary halo. It leases shared geometry/materials/blend textures and owns spatial picking proxies independently of draw batches. `utils/mapRenderResources.ts` supplies preview caching/disposal. `useMapAutosave` calls `useMap.updateMapMinimal` and leaves all live-root ownership in EncounterMap. No new package or stored field.

**2026-11-09d ramp geometry:** `MapCanvas.vue` imports `utils/voxelGeometry.ts` to create block or ramp geometry for map meshes and drawing previews. Ramp direction comes from `useMapEditor` through `EncounterMap` and `MapToolbar`. **(2026-11-09e)** The canvas's capture-phase `onCanvasWheel` emits `rotate-ramp(-1|1)` to the editor's `rotateRampDirection`; `nextTick` refreshes the hover/drag preview after prop synchronization. The toolbar also advertises wheel rotation. The helper depends on Three.js and the `MapVoxel` type; no new package. Transparent ramp meshes retain their own geometry and edges instead of entering `buildVoxelSurface`'s cube merging.

**2026-11-09b:** `MapRadialChoiceAction`/`MapRadialOptions` add optional `special-order` children. Canvas preserves colon-containing Special/Skill Order identities through `action-selected`; consumers without children retain the existing `player-action` event. The GM page reuses `specialOrderTargets`, Special/Skill Order cost/usage helpers, Mode Change helpers, Willpower evolution, existing clash actions and `chebyshev` Direct range checks. Normal Move still flows through EncounterMap's paid movement endpoint; GM Move remains its separate free repositioning flow. No new package, endpoint, storage field or environment setting.

**2026-11-09a:** `combatAttackOptions` supplies shared Complex costs, Shield availability and effect-option validation to the radial and declaration endpoints. New pure `utils/lifesteal.ts` supplies capped healing to NPC/player/legacy/intercede resolution. New `server/utils/positiveSupportEffect.ts` applies Shield temp Wounds and Haste action credits consistently. `applyEffect` rolls/schedules Lag; new pure `utils/lagInitiative.ts` applies/restores turn slots in client turn changes/removals, encounter PUT and shared/legacy knockout round wraps, using existing batched participant-record loading for grouped partners. Intercede damage uses existing `applyWoundDamage` so temporary Wounds absorb damage before Combat Monster accumulation. No new packages or environment settings.

**2026-11-09:** `AttackRadialRider` includes `charge-before`/`charge-after`, and `RadialAttackSelection` carries the resolved `ChargeAttackMode`. The shared catalog validates the Charge tag and rejects conflicting timing choices. Both page adapters use `buildMapAttackDescriptor` to pass `attackId` and timing through to `EncounterMap`, whose watcher reuses its existing Charge handlers. No server request, schema, migration or package changes.

**2026-11-08u:** Pure `utils/tamerExperience.reconcileTamerExperience` replaces `nextTotalXp` and returns both the calculated wallet and budget. The form and Tamer PUT share it. Tamer POST/PUT reuse `server/utils/campaignDm` for budget authorization; PUT uses a Drizzle row-lock transaction matching rewards' Tamer locks. No new packages, environment variables or storage fields.

**2026-11-08t:** Both reward endpoints import pure `utils/rewardPartners.ts`. Its iterative grouping handles direct links, evolution-line chains and legacy JSON strings, then chooses a stable root representative and shared Bonus DP maximum. POST reads/locks the selected Tamers' campaign Digimon and evolution lines before resolving partner awards. `types/rewards.ts` adds optional receipt metadata and affected-form results; the schema only changes its JSONB TypeScript annotation. No new packages or environment settings.

**2026-11-08s:** `types/rewards.ts` defines roster/request/result shapes and pure `utils/rewards.ts` normalizes and compares grants. The reward page uses `useRewardGrant`; GET/POST use `server/utils/campaignDm.ts`, which combines `campaignAuth` account roles with signed GM-password cookies (Node crypto and h3, no new package or env secret). Existing password verification issues the credential using the campaign's private password hash as its signing key. POST uses Drizzle transactions/row locks and `serializeIntercedeClaim` queues, then `encounterRoom.broadcast`. Reward receipts use the new schema table. Both combat pages reuse existing record/snapshot reload helpers; the player adds a campaign WebSocket subscription.

**2026-11-08r:** The player page and GM cards reuse `useTamers.calculateDerivedStats.maxInspiration`. New pure `utils/tamerExperience.ts` validates lifetime values and reconciles available/allocated XP for Tamer API callers that omit `totalXp`, using arithmetic rank-price sums and stored Torment receipts. The form tracks explicit awards separately. Bundle/library serializers carry the nullable field. `MapCanvas` supplies its existing participants to `withRadialActionCosts` for payer labeling; `MapPlayerHUD` already receives action balances. No new packages or environment settings.

**2026-11-08p:** `attackRadialOptions` composes `attackBadges`, `combatAttackOptions`, `specialOrderCombat` and campaign Inspiration costs. `combatAttackOptions.attackActionCost` uses the existing area-shape parser and is shared by the radial display and direct-GM intercede endpoint. `MapCanvas` imports pure `radialActionCosts`; `MapRadialMenu` extends its row layout with cost text. The GM page adds `usePendingAttackFateful`, which supplies a local roll target to existing `useFatefulIntervention`. No new packages, database fields or environment settings.

**2026-11-08o:** `resolveAreaIntercedeGroup` receives optional map/position/campaign context and returns an optional aggregate position patch. It uses `resolveInstantSupportEffect` for hit interceptors and the canonical NPC resolver for uncovered instant-support recipients. `resolveNpcAttack` and support response handling share that instant resolver. `pushPull.ts` adds an explicit deferred spatial mode, accepting the caller's map/positions/records and returning displacement plus knockout-gravity patches without room writes. The original room wrapper remains available to ordinary response paths. No packages or schema changes.

**2026-11-08n:** The GM map adds the already-shared `MapRadialOptions`/`MapRadialChoiceAction` types and connects the existing `MapCanvas -> EncounterMap -> action-selected` event to its evolution handler. No server, package, schema or evolution-rule changes.

**2026-11-08l:** Pure `utils/cleanse.ts` owns selection limits, candidate filtering and immutable duration reduction. `server/utils/cleanse.ts` initializes casts, records target/interceptor outcomes and prunes empty requests. Intercede creation/decisions/claims, NPC attack resolution, submitted defenses and the legacy endpoint share those helpers. Both pages use `useCleanseSelection -> actions/cleanse` and `CleanseDialog`. `MapCanvas` imports pure `utils/movementGap.ts`, caching sorted floor/voxel heights by column in a map-only computed; each hover uses a binary search rather than scanning or serializing the map. No packages or map-size limits are added.

**2026-11-08k:** GM management adds the `MapCanvas -> EncounterMap -> encounter page` `gm-manage` event and passes source options from the page into `EffectManager`. The page reuses `applyDerivedStatEffects` for source SPEC values. Shared `combatDice` adds the target-dependent Fear penalty. No packages or storage fields are added.

**2026-11-08j:** `intercedeDecision.ts` adds `readCombatRecord` from the existing request-local cache for legacy pending-definition recovery; area resolution already imports it. Deferred support routing consumes server attack metadata rather than relying on a client's roll-only payload. No packages are added.

**2026-11-08i:** `MapCanvas.vue` reuses its existing `utils/voxelSurface.ts` dependency for AoE previews as well as transparent map voxels. Shape generation and line-of-sight classification still feed the renderer unchanged; no package or API dependency is added.

**2026-11-08h occupancy policy:** `utils/movementRules.ts` owns reciprocal `sizeInteraction` and `getMovementOccupancy` (separate traversal-blocked and landing-blocked cell sets). `useMapMovement.ts`, `utils/placementRules.ts`, `MapCanvas.vue` and `server/utils/mapMovement.ts` consume this policy; the server helper accepts a mover and a landing/traversal purpose. Intercede offers/claims, Clash Throw and push/pull supply these size-aware sets. The chooser reuses `MapRadialMenu.vue`; no package or storage dependency is added.

**2026-11-08g path selection:** `useMapMovement.ts` consumes `utils/pathfinding.ts`'s retained BFS tree and A* fallback. Both searches rank routes by movement steps first, then vertical travel. BFS updates equal-depth predecessors before the next depth is processed, retaining one queue visit per discovered cell and cached hover paths. No new import, package or server movement-rule dependency is introduced.

> 2026-11-08d: `partnerDevolution.ts` exports `autoDevolveParticipant`, using the existing record-cache scope, `resolveCombatDevolutionState`, participant naming and caller-supplied select/update database. It is consumed by `intercede-claim`, `resolveAreaIntercedeGroup`, `responses`, `npc-attack`, `resolveNpcAttack`, encounter PUT and `enemyKnockouts`; those modules no longer own copied restoration/logging blocks. `resolveCombatEvolutionIndex` in the pure evolution utility is shared by manual evolution, partner choices and server fallback resolution.

> 2026-11-08c: `utils/combatEvolutionOptions.getCombatEvolutionParent` is reused by the client builder and new `server/utils/partnerDevolution.ts`. The server exports a non-mutating `resolveCombatDevolutionState` and `buildCombatDevolutionState`, reusing cached record reads, stage configuration and Digizoid health. Attack/response/intercede/PUT/knockout callers pass their own database (including claim transactions), consume history only after resolution, and retain their existing writes, logs and aura refreshes.

> 2026-11-08b: `combatRecordCache.readCombatRecords` shares the existing per-ID Promise caches with single-record/derived readers. `combatSpatial.loadParticipantDigimon` and `loadTamersByIds` supply bulk SQL adapters. Guiding Light and round/encounter-start triggers use these loaders; encounter PUT and enemy devolution use cached single reads for remaining lookups. The PUT establishes the outer request scope. Raw entity/derived records alone are cached; participant effects and mutations remain live.

> 2026-11-08: Both encounter pages import `utils/mapAttackDescriptor.ts` (which reuses `data/attackConstants.applyDerivedStatEffects`) and dependency-free `utils/combatEvolutionOptions.ts`; map components import `MapAttackDescriptor` type-only. Five server action routes import `server/utils/participantTurn.ts`, which reuses `combatRecordCache.readCombatRecord` through a caller-supplied database query. All new model imports are type-only.

> 2026-11-07o: `types/combat.ts` imports `Stance`/`Vec3` type-only; `types/index.ts` and `useEncounters.ts` re-export canonical combat types, and the schema imports its derived storage alias. No runtime dependency cycle is introduced. Both encounter pages import pure `utils/combatAttackOptions.ts`, which reuses `specialOrderCombat.signatureReadyRound` and imports shared rule/model types only.

> 2026-11-07n: `useDigimonForm.ts` and `useDigimonStats.ts` import pure `utils/movementQualities.ts`. `MapCanvas.vue`, `MapPlayerHUD.vue`, `encounters/[id].vue` and `player/[tamerId].vue` import pure `utils/participantDisplayNames.ts`. The player page also reuses its existing `useTamers` calculator. `server/utils/participantName.ts` stays separate from the client helper (it groups by entity ID) but now applies the same enemy-only rule. These helpers introduce no new packages.

> 2026-11-07m: `MapRadialMenu.vue` uses the geometry/measurement types exported by `utils/radialMenu.ts`; the helper remains independent of the DOM through an injected text-measure function. The component measures fonts with the browser's Canvas and Font Loading APIs. No package changes.

> 2026-11-07l: `utils/attackBadges.ts` now also imports the `MapRadialItem` type and exports `attackRadialItem`, which both encounter pages (`encounters/[id].vue`, `player/[tamerId].vue`) use for map radial attack entries; `MapRadialMenu.vue` imports `radialDetailRows` and the `RadialRowKind` type from `utils/radialMenu.ts`. No new packages.

> 2026-11-07h: `MapCanvas.vue` auto-imports `MapRadialMenu.vue`; the renderer imports pure `utils/radialMenu.ts` for sector paths, inward fan fitting and label wrapping. `types/mapRadial.ts` defines menu items/options/choice-action types shared by the renderer, map bridge and both encounter pages. No new packages or server imports.

> 2026-11-07j: `EncounterMap.vue` imports pure `utils/placementRules.ts`. `MapCanvas.vue` imports `utils/placementRules.ts` (`evaluatePlacement`) and `utils/tokenDrag.ts`, which imports `createDragTracker` from `placementRules` and nothing else. `placementRules.ts` has only a type import (`~/types`). `MapPlayerHUD.vue` gains no imports. No new packages or server imports.

> 2026-11-07g: Attack preparation joins `intercedeClaims.serializeIntercedeClaim`; intercede-offer delegation must remain outside that non-reentrant queue. `combatDodge.recountSubmittedDodge` uses the shared defense and `areaDefense.getPaidDodgeDiceBonus`, with payment metadata recorded by the queued spend-inspiration route and recovered by both pages and `useAreaDefenseBatch`.

> 2026-11-07f: `combatDice` depends on raw stat swaps, stance/effect rules and derived stats; `areaDefense` and `server/utils/combatDodge` share the Dodge formula. `attackAccuracy` carries success thresholds, automatic successes and power suppression through rolls/rerolls. `specialOrderCombat` owns declaration snapshots/consumption, signature readiness and Enduring Soul; `intercedeActions` owns next-round Special Order debt. `strikeLast` creates automatic-miss offers and later counter prompts. `combatRecordCache` wraps area/intercede/response resolution and raw derived loads; record writes invalidate cached entities. `pathfinding.searchReachable` provides predecessors reused by `useMapMovement`, whose context cache is invalidated by `EncounterMap` before relevant map/occupancy/character changes.
> Last verified: 2026-04-14
> 2026-11-07e: **Pacing race fixes.** `intercedeClaims` now also orders full offer creation, request creation and request/response DELETEs. Quick Reaction calls `intercedePasses` after clearing exhausted flags; `resolvePassedOffers` re-evaluates `isOfferPassed` against the latest resolution state. Both pages use `combatRecords.createRecordRefreshQueue`; `encounterSnapshot.snapshotRevision` reads the database-trigger token. `RollInterceptPanel` accepts an async spend callback instead of an event, letting its three player usages await payment. No package changes.
> 2026-11-07d: **Area defense panel.** New dependency-light `utils/areaDefense.ts` imports `utils/statSwaps.ts`, `utils/stanceModifiers.ts`, `utils/clashRules.ts` and `data/attackConstants.ts` (`getEffectStatModifiers`); used by `player/[tamerId].vue` (which no longer imports `clashRules` itself) and `composables/useAreaDefenseBatch.ts` (type only). `useAreaDefenseBatch.ts` imports only `vue`; `components/AreaDefensePanel.vue` imports its types. All three are used only by `player/[tamerId].vue`. `server/utils/resolveAreaIntercedeGroup.ts` gains one field and no imports.
> 2026-11-07c: **Temporary intercede passes.** `utils/intercedeOptOuts.ts` gains `TemporaryIntercedePass`, `findPassSlot`, `getActiveTemporaryPass(es)`, `getPassingOwnerIds`, `offerHasQuickReaction`, `isOfferPassed`, `isOfferSuppressed` (still import-free) and is now also read by `server/utils/intercedePasses.ts`, `composables/useIntercedePass.ts` and `intercede-pass.post.ts` (via the server util). `utils/intercedeActions.ts` gains `recordTurnStart`, imported by the same three turn-start paths as `applyDeferredIntercedeCost`. New `server/utils/intercedePasses.ts` (`applyActivePasses`, `passableOfferOwners`) imports `db`/`encounters`, `utils/intercedeOptOuts` and `./intercedeDecision`; imported by `intercede-pass.post.ts` and `intercede-offer.post.ts`. `server/utils/intercedeOptOuts.ts` gains `setTemporaryPass`/`clearTemporaryPass`/`clearTemporaryPasses` (also imported by `[id].put.ts`); `intercedeDecision.ts` gains `resolvePassedOffers`. `intercede-offer.post.ts` newly imports `intercedePasses` and `intercedeClaims`. New `IntercedePassNotice.vue` and `useIntercedePass.ts` are used only by `player/[tamerId].vue`.
> 2026-11-07b: **Targeted encounter updates.** New dependency-free `utils/encounterSnapshot.ts` (snapshot gate, `shapeActionResponse`, `trimBattleLog`, `normalizeActionsRemaining`, `newBattleLogEntries`, `needsFullReload`), imported by `server/utils/encounterPayload.ts` (`~/utils`-relative), `encounters/[id].vue` and `player/[tamerId].vue`. New `utils/combatRecords.ts` (`planRecordRefresh`, `ACTION_RECORD_HINTS`, `mergeRecordsById`) imports only `utils/evolutionForms.ts` and is imported by both pages. `composables/useMapWebSocket.ts` gains `onReconnect`. `utils/evolutionForms.ts` is no longer imported by the pages directly.
> 2026-11-06f: **Intercede decision ordering.** New dependency-free `server/utils/intercedeClaims.ts` exports `serializeIntercedeClaim`, imported by the claim, Skip, opt-out and Quick Reaction handlers. The claim handler passes its transaction as optional `database` to `resolveAreaIntercedeGroup`, whose existing callers retain the default database. Claim knockouts use the supplied-map gravity path and collect its returned positions until commit.
> 2026-11-06e: **Deferred intercede actions.** New dependency-free `utils/intercedeActions.ts` exports typed `applyDeferredIntercedeCost`, imported by `composables/useEncounters.ts`, `server/utils/enemyKnockouts.ts` and `server/api/encounters/[id]/actions/npc-attack.post.ts`. Each turn-start path applies it to the active participant and any existing grouped partner.
> 2026-11-03p: **Prepared Scene tabs.** New `server/api/campaigns/[id]/scene-presets.get.ts`/`scene-presets.put.ts`, importing only `db`/`campaigns` from `server/db` (the PUT also takes `MAX_SCENE_PRESETS`/`ScenePreset` from `types/index.ts`). No `broadcast`, no new util. `ScenePanel.vue` now also imports `MAX_SCENE_PRESETS`/`ScenePreset` from `types/index.ts`, and `server/db/schema.ts` imports the `ScenePreset` type for `campaigns.scenePresets`.
> 2026-11-03o: **Enemy knockouts.** New `server/utils/enemyKnockouts.ts` (`knockedOutEnemyIds`, `resolveEnemyKnockouts`, `hasUnusedTimeControl`, `triggerTimeControl`), imported by `[id].put.ts`, `resolveNpcAttack.ts`, `move.post.ts`, `clash-action.post.ts`, `intercede-claim.post.ts`, `resolveAreaIntercedeGroup.ts`, `responses.post.ts`, `pushPull.ts` and `npc-attack.post.ts`. It depends on `endOfTurnGravity.ts`, `roundStartQualityTriggers.ts`, `damageOverTime.ts`, `combatSpatial.ts`, `resolveSupportAttack.ts`, `applyEffect.ts` and `participantName.ts`, so `resolveNpcAttack.ts` no longer imports `endOfTurnGravity`, `roundStartQualityTriggers` or `getDigimonDerivedStats` itself. No import cycle.
> 2026-11-03h: **Live evolution options.** New pure `app/utils/evolutionForms.ts` (`evolutionFormsKey`), imported by `encounters/[id].vue` and `player/[tamerId].vue` (`~/utils` imports) — decides when their WS/poll refresh paths refetch evolution lines. No imports of its own.
> 2026-11-03f: **Shared line of sight + doors.** New pure `app/utils/lineOfSight.ts` (`MAP_GEOMETRY`, `buildLosIndex`, `losClear`, `losClip`, `canSeeFootprint`, `segmentCells`; imports `mapVoxels.ts` (`getMapVoxels`, new `voxelBlocksTargeting`) and `movementRules.ts` (`getFootprintCells`)), imported by `MapCanvas.vue` and `EncounterMap.vue`. New pure `app/utils/mapDoors.ts` (`wallFaceKey`, `doorsAdjacentTo`, `closedDoorsCrossedByPath`; imports `movementRules.ts`), imported by `MapCanvas.vue` and `EncounterMap.vue`. Removed the unused `hasLineOfSight` (plus `rayBoxIntersect`/`rayAABB` and their `mapVoxels` imports) from `utils/mapGeometry.ts` and `server/utils/gridDistance.ts`; their other exports are unchanged.
> 2026-11-03d: **No outside-clash dodge, from live state.** New pure `app/utils/clashRules.ts` (`isAttackedFromOutsideClash`), imported by `server/utils/clashArmor.ts`, `server/utils/resolveNpcAttack.ts`, `responses.post.ts`, `intercede-offer.post.ts` (server, relative or `~/utils` imports) and by `encounters/[id].vue` + `player/[tamerId].vue` (`~/utils/clashRules`). No request-data shape changed — the existing `cannotDodge` flag is kept and OR'd in.
> 2026-11-03c: **Outside-clash CPU armor.** New `server/utils/clashArmor.ts` (`getOutsideClashArmorBonus`), imported by `computeAttackDamage.ts` and `responses.post.ts`; depends only on `resolveSupportAttack.ts` (`getDigimonDerivedStats`). `computeAttackDamage()` gains a required `participants` param (its two callers, `resolveNpcAttack.ts` and `intercede-claim.post.ts`, pass theirs). The `outsideClashCpuPenalty` field is gone from `attack.post` → `intercede-offer` → request data → `resolveNpcAttack`/`responses.post`/`quick-reaction`/`resolveAreaIntercedeGroup`/`triggerCounterattack`.
> 2026-08-19: **Ambient scene image.** New `server/api/campaigns/[id]/scene.get.ts`/`scene.put.ts`, importing `db`/`campaigns` from `server/db` plus `broadcast` (from `server/utils/encounterRoom.ts`) and `campaignRoomKey` (from `server/utils/rollLog.ts`) — no new server util, just reuses the roll-log feature's existing `campaign:{id}` room plumbing. New `components/ScenePanel.vue` (mounted in `app.vue` alongside `RollLogPanel`), depends on `composables/useMapWebSocket.ts` (`basePath: 'campaigns'`, unchanged) the same way `RollLogPanel.vue` does.
> 2026-08-19: **Jump-to-air landings (Jumper can finish a move — or a Charge [Pass] — in mid-air).** `utils/movementRules.ts`: `canLandOn()` gained an **optional trailing `origin?: Vec3`** param plus a private `canJumpTo(pos, origin, caps)` helper; both air branches (painted air space tile, and open unpainted air within the map footprint) now accept `caps.canFly || canJumpTo(...)` instead of `caps.canFly` alone, capping the climb at `caps.jumpHeight` (level/descending jumps pass trivially). Omitting `origin` preserves the old fliers-only behaviour, so no caller breaks by default. Both real callers were threaded: `composables/useMapMovement.ts` `computeReachable()` passes its own `origin` (this is what makes ordinary jump movement reach air cells), and `MapCanvas.vue`'s `isPassLandingValid()`/`anyPassLandingValid()` gained an `attackerPos` param forwarded to `canLandOn`. **`MapCanvas.vue` lost its local `getAttackerCapabilities()`** (it called `detectCapabilities(quals, 0, 0, 0)`, hard-zeroing `jumpRange`/`jumpHeight`, and its `digimonMap` prop is the trimmed canvas map with no `baseStats`) — caps now arrive as a new `attackerCaps` prop from `EncounterMap.vue`, whose `attackerCaps` computed reuses `npcMoveCaps()` so the Pass landing rule and the movement-range rule agree by construction. `MapCanvas.vue` also dropped its now-unused `detectCapabilities` import. Server: `combatSpatial.ts` `getFallerProfile()` now **derives** `airborneByJump` (`explicit flag || has the extra-movement/jumper quality`) rather than reading a flag nothing sets — safe because every displacement path (`pushPull`, clash throw, intercede throw) calls `resolveFall` immediately at the point of displacement, so anything still airborne at end-of-turn gravity got there under its own movement. Net effect: a Jumper ending its turn mid-air falls via the existing `endOfTurnGravity` path and takes **0** fall damage (`resolveFall` short-circuits on `airborneByJump`). No signature change to `resolveFall`, `getReachableCells`, or `computeGravityDrops`.
> 2026-07-08: **Campaign-wide roll history.** New `server/utils/rollLog.ts` (`appendRollLogEntry(campaignId, entry)` — insert, prune to newest 50 per campaign, broadcast; imports `broadcast` from `encounterRoom.ts`), consumed by new `server/api/campaigns/[id]/rolls.get.ts`/`rolls.post.ts` and by `new-day.post.ts` (now also drops a `kind:'new-day'` marker row). New `server/api/campaigns/[id]/ws.ts` reuses `encounterRoom.ts`'s `joinRoom`/`leaveRoom` under a `campaign:{id}` room key (previously encounter-only room keys) — no changes to `encounterRoom.ts` itself, since room keys were already arbitrary strings. Client: `composables/useMapWebSocket.ts` gained a `basePath: 'encounters'|'campaigns'` param (default preserves all existing callers); new `components/RollLogPanel.vue` (no deps beyond `useMapWebSocket`) is mounted globally in `app.vue`, and `player/[tamerId].vue`'s `performAttributeRoll`/`performTormentRoll` gained a `logRoll()` fire-and-forget POST to the new endpoint.
> Graph changes: added `computeAttackDamage.ts` (new server util extracted ~Apr 12, imported by `intercede-claim.post` and `resolveNpcAttack`); added `server/middleware/encounter-sync.ts` (new global Nitro middleware, broadcasts `encounter-updated` via `encounterRoom.notifyEncounterUpdated` after any successful `POST /api/encounters/[id]/actions/*`)
> 2026-06-13: added `server/utils/encounterPayload.ts` (new server util, `buildEncounterPayload()`, used by `[id].get.ts` and `encounter-sync.ts`'s `encounter-state` broadcast); added `server/middleware/01-rate-limit.ts` (new global Nitro middleware, runs before `encounter-sync.ts`, no further deps)
> 2026-06-13b: `encounter-sync.ts` now also matches `PUT /api/encounters/[id]` (in addition to `POST .../actions/*`), so no new deps but its trigger surface covers the bare encounter PUT (`nextTurn`/`endCombat`/`updateEncounter()`); no other dependency changes.
> 2026-06-15: `encounter-sync.ts`'s trigger regex generalized from enumerated `POST .../actions/*` + bare `PUT /api/encounters/[id]` to any `POST`/`PUT`/`DELETE` matching `/^\/api\/encounters\/([^/?]+)(?:\/.*)?$/` — no new deps, but now also covers `requests.post`, `responses.post`, `requests/[requestId].delete`, and `responses/[responseId].delete`.
> 2026-06-16: `encounter-sync.ts` WS broadcast now uses `Date.now()` as the `version` field instead of `encounter.version` (`room.version`). `room.version` only increments on map events, so it stayed at 0 throughout Setup/non-map phases — allowing out-of-order WS pushes to overwrite newer state and causing "Request not found" errors when the GM processed player responses. No dep changes.
> 2026-06-15b: added `server/utils/selectiveTargeting.ts` (new server util, pure helpers `getSelectiveTargetingFilter`/`selectiveTargetingBlocksDamage`/`selectiveTargetingBlocksEffect`, no deps beyond `EFFECT_ALIGNMENT` from `data/attackConstants.ts`); imported by `computeAttackDamage.ts`, `resolveSupportAttack.ts`, `responses.post.ts`, `resolveAreaIntercedeGroup.ts`, and `intercede-offer.post.ts`.
> 2026-06-17: Knockback/Pull instant effects now displace targets on the map. `mapMovement.ts` gained `findPushPullLandingCell()` (walks target horizontally away/toward attacker by `CPU+Stage` cells, uses `PROJECTILE_CAPS` so target can be pushed off ledges, stops at first blocked cell, returns furthest valid position). `responses.post.ts` applies displacement after `dodge-rolled` hit resolution when `appliedEffectName` is `'Knockback'`/`'Pull'` and `encounter.mapId` is set. `resolveNpcAttack.ts` extended with optional `mapRecord`/`participantPositions` params and now returns `positionPatch?: Record<string, Vec3>`; `intercede-offer.post.ts` (both NPC call sites) and `intercede-skip.post.ts` (new map loading block + NPC call site) apply the returned patch via `applyPositionPatch` + `broadcast`. Fall damage mirrors Clash Throw logic. Deferred: `resolveAreaIntercedeGroup.ts`, `triggerCounterattack.ts`, counterattack path in `responses.post.ts`.
> 2026-07-03: `findPushPullLandingCell()` reworked to be 3D and size-aware. Now takes an extra `attackerDims` arg (both callers `responses.post.ts`/`resolveNpcAttack.ts` compute it via `getFootprintDimsForParticipant`); direction is derived from footprint **centers** (not anchors); movement steps in x/y/z (away for Push, toward for Pull). Walls/solid voxels/ceilings now stop it (per-step `canPassThrough` over the target's whole footprint), water/wind never block, and a grounded target can't be pushed down through the floor (new private helpers `footprintCenter`/`hasSolidFootingBelow`/`footprintCanStep`). Pull self-limits on the attacker's occupied footprint.
> 2026-07-29b: **Fix — Knockback/Pull distance = CPU Value + Stage Bonus.** The push distance was just the CPU spec value; the rule adds the stage bonus on top of it. Since `DigimonDerivedStats.cpu` already = `floor(Body/10) + stageBonus`, `calculateEffectPotency` ([resolveSupportAttack.ts](app/server/utils/resolveSupportAttack.ts)) now adds the attacker's stage bonus again for `Knockback`/`Pull` (e.g. a rookie with CPU 1 → 1 + 1 = 2 tiles). Added a `stageBonus` field to `DigimonDerivedStats` / `calculateDigimonDerivedStats` (types/index.ts) so the potency calc can read it. Fixes all four push paths (`responses.post`, `resolveNpcAttack` via `computeAttackDamage`, `resolveInstantSupportEffect`, `npc-attack.post`).
> 2026-07-29: **Fix — maps are endless (map size no longer blocks knockback/movement).** `utils/movementRules.ts`: `isWithinMapFootprint` now returns `true` unconditionally, so open-air flight/knockback is bounded only by move budget / distance, not by `map.dimensions` (which stays for the starting floor + camera centering). This fixes push/pull returning null when a target would cross the painted-floor edge. `settleFootprintY` reworked: a fall now rests on the highest support at or below the unit's level **including floors below the base level** (new `lowestStructureY` bound), and a unit over an open column **stays put** instead of teleporting to y=0 or falling forever — so a knock-off into a pit/lower floor drops onto it (via the push fall and end-of-turn gravity), while a knock-off into empty space just hovers at level. No data-model/UI changes.
> 2026-07-04c: **Feature — GM free-move (reposition any character, any turn).** Client-only. `MapCanvas.vue` gained a `gmMoveParticipantId` prop, `gm-move`/`gm-moved` emits, a GM-only "GM Move" radial button (on the enemy combat radial + a new minimal `gmRadial` that the DM can open on any token — player-owned or off-turn NPC — regardless of turn), and the Setup placement click handler now also honors `gmMoveParticipantId` (free teleport onto any valid tile, same footprint/blocking-voxel/occupancy validation). `EncounterMap.vue` added a `gmMoveParticipantId` ref + `onGmMoveStart`/`onGmMove` handlers that reposition via a WS `unit-moved` broadcast with **no** `/actions/move` call → no action cost, no distance limit. No server changes (the WS `unit-moved` handler never validated turn/ownership).
> 2026-07-04b: **Fix — Knockback/Pull also weren't displacing on NPC-initiated attacks.** `npc-attack.post.ts` (the NPC→player endpoint) resolves attacks inline (it doesn't call `resolveNpcAttack`) and its support **and** damage blocks applied the effect but never displaced. Both blocks now call `applyPushPullDisplacement` after the effect is applied (distance = attacker CPU+Stage via `calculateEffectPotency`), threading the updated participants + a `displaced N cell(s)` note into their dodge log. Player-initiated attacks were already covered: `attack.post.ts` delegates to `intercede-offer` → `resolveNpcAttack` (NPC target) or dodge-roll → `responses.post.ts` (player target). Damage Knockback still requires a 2+ wound hit.
> 2026-07-04: **Fix — Knockback/Pull never displaced on support moves.** The support-attack resolution branches (`intercede-offer.post.ts` area + single-target NPC support, `intercede-skip.post.ts`) only handled `positive-auto`/`positive-health`/`negative` resolution types and silently dropped `'instant'` effects — but `getEffectResolutionType('Knockback'/'Pull')` returns `'instant'` (they're in `INSTANT_EFFECTS`), so the effect + displacement were never applied. New `server/utils/pushPull.ts`: `computePushPull` (pure core), `applyPushPullDisplacement` (room wrapper — load map/positions/digimon → `findPushPullLandingCell` → `resolveFall` → `broadcastPositionPatch`), and `resolveInstantSupportEffect` (support-resolver-shaped: computes distance = attacker CPU+Stage via `calculateEffectPotency`, displaces, returns `{ participants, battleLog, …, resolved }`). The two damage-path push blocks (`responses.post.ts`, `resolveNpcAttack.ts`) now call the shared core instead of inlining it; each support-resolution site gained an `else if (resolutionType === 'instant')` branch. Player-target support Knockback already routed to a dodge-roll → `responses.post.ts` (works). Damage-move Knockback still (correctly) requires a 2+ wound hit.
> 2026-07-03e: **Modularisation pass — Phase 3 (falling module).** `utils/movementRules.ts` gained pure `resolveFall(startPos, dims, map, profile) → { landingPos, fallHeight, damage }` + `FallerProfile`; `server/utils/combatSpatial.ts` gained `getFallerProfile(participant, digimonById)` (CPU/RAM + Tumbler/adv-jumper/Flight/airborneByJump; tamers CPU 1). The four hand-rolled fall sites (push/pull in `responses.post`/`resolveNpcAttack`, Clash Throw in `clash-action.post`, thrown-ally in `intercede-claim.post`) and `endOfTurnGravity.computeGravityDrops` now all call `getFallerProfile` + `resolveFall`. **Two behavior corrections from unifying on `settleFootprintY`:** (1) fall landing/height on ground-tile floors is fixed (the old push/pull/clash/thrown scan floated units one cell above the floor; `settleFootprintY` matches actual placement — `tile.y` for ground, `voxel.y+1` for voxels), so those falls now land correctly and may deal +1 more; (2) Flight now consistently exempts a displaced/thrown unit from fall damage everywhere (Clash Throw and thrown-ally previously damaged flyers). No change to the fall formula itself.
> 2026-07-03d: **Modularisation pass — Phase 2 (spatial loaders + patch broadcast).** New `server/utils/combatSpatial.ts` with `loadEncounterMap(mapId)` (full GameMap incl. spaceTiles/ceilings/dimensions), `loadParticipantDigimon(participants)` (digimonById), and `getMovementProfile(participant, digimonById, tamerById)` (caps+budget+bodyStat). `encounterRoom.ts` gained `broadcastPositionPatch(encounterId, patch)` (applyPositionPatch + `position-patch` broadcast) and `getDestroyedStructureIds(destructibleStates)`. Refactored the ~6 map-load sites, ~5 digimonById sites, `intercede-offer`'s inline `movementProfileFor` + `intercede-claim`'s two inline caps/profile blocks, and all 8 position-patch broadcast pairs (`responses.post`, `resolveNpcAttack`, `endOfTurnGravity`, `intercede-offer/claim/skip.post`, `clash-action.post`) onto the shared helpers. **Behavior note:** the previously-partial map records (push/pull in `responses.post`, gravity, `intercede-skip` push/pull) now load complete maps, so server pathing there respects ceilings + space tiles (matching the client and the already-complete `intercede-offer/claim`/`clash-action` maps). No fall-number changes.
> 2026-07-03c: **Modularisation pass — Phase 1 (geometry + capability unification).** `utils/movementRules.ts` is now the single source of truth for map geometry and movement capabilities. Moved `detectCapabilities`, `PROJECTILE_CAPS`, `isPositionInAir`, `computeFallDamage`, `isFootprintAirborne`, `settleFootprintY` into it (and exported `hasSolidVoxelSupport`/`hasSolidStairSupport`). `server/utils/mapMovement.ts` deleted its duplicate `canPassThrough`/wall/ceiling/voxel/stair/caps copies and now imports + re-exports the shared ones (keeps only the server pathfinding helpers: `getReachableCells`, the `find*` finders, `buildFootprintOccupiedSet`, `getFootprintDimsForParticipant`, `isValidLandingPosition`/`isFootprintValid`); `detectCapabilitiesFromQualities` is an alias re-export. `composables/useMapMovement.ts` dropped its local `detectCapabilities`/`PROJECTILE_CAPS` copies and re-exports from `movementRules`. **Behavior reconciliation:** server map pathing now uses the client's stricter `canPassThrough` (diagonal wall-corner cuts blocked, vertical ground step-up gated by fly/jump) — matching what the client already renders. Shared geometry is defensive against server map records that omit `spaceTiles`/`ceilings` (completed in Phase 2). Finders gained an optional `destroyed: Set<string>` param (default empty; real wiring in Phase 2). No fall-number changes.
> 2026-07-03b: **End-of-turn gravity + corrected fall-damage rule.** New util `server/utils/endOfTurnGravity.ts` (`computeGravityDrops` pure core + `applyEndOfTurnGravity` room wrapper) drops every airborne, non-flying participant to the surface and applies fall damage at each true turn boundary. `mapMovement.ts` gained `computeFallDamage`/`isFootprintAirborne`/`settleFootprintY`. **Fall damage is now `max(1, (fallHeight − 5) − CPU)` for falls past 5m (0 otherwise)**, Tumbler adds `RAM×2` reduction and Advanced Mobility: Jumper negates — applied at **every** fall site (`endOfTurnGravity`, push/pull in `responses.post.ts`/`resolveNpcAttack.ts`, Clash Throw in `clash-action.post.ts`, thrown-ally in `intercede-claim.post.ts`). Push/pull now Flight-exempt (flyers hover at the pushed cell). Interceptor jump-to-intercede fall damage is now 0 (jump-into-air exemption). Gravity fires from `[id].put.ts` (client turn-end, before auto-devolve) and the defeated-active-NPC branches of `npc-attack.post.ts` + `resolveNpcAttack.ts` (merged into `positionPatch`); KO re-alignment index shifts are excluded. New `CombatParticipant.airborneByJump?: boolean` flag (prep for future jump-to-air-tile landing: such units still fall at end of turn but take 0 damage and the flag clears; nothing sets it yet).
> 2026-06-15c: `selectiveTargeting.ts` gained `selectiveTargetingExcludesTarget()` (decides if a target is *fully* shielded). `intercede-offer.post.ts` now drops fully-shielded `[Area]` targets up front (no dodge prompt / intercede offer / NPC resolve) and returns `selectiveTargetingExcludedIds` on the encounter payload; consumed by the player page (`player/[tamerId].vue`, shrinks the AoE result-modal count) and the GM page (`encounters/[id].vue`, trims logged target names). No new module deps.

**Sources:** All files traced above.

### Top-Level Module Relationships

The GM/player pages share `useDigivolveWillpower` (Vue + campaign DC; calls the existing digivolve/failure APIs), `useClashAttack` (Vue + `areaShapes` + `attackAccuracy`; injected submission/map adapters), and `utils/attackAccuracy.ts` (pure dice/rider preparation). Both shared dialogs emit typed user intents; their type-only composable imports do not import database/server modules at runtime. Tests load these modules and compile the actual SFC dialogs through `tests/helpers/loadTypeScript.mjs`.

```mermaid
flowchart TD
    App["app.vue (root)"]
    Layouts["layouts/\ndefault.vue, player.vue"]
    Pages["pages/**\n(25+ routes)"]
    Components["components/\n(14 shared)"]
    Composables["composables/\n(17 shared)"]
    Types["types/index.ts\n(all types + constants)"]
    Data["data/\nqualities, attacks, hazards, special-orders"]
    Utils["utils/\ndisplayHelpers, formDefaults, stanceModifiers, specialOrders, torment-validation, areaShapes, movementRules, statSwaps, modeChange, creationRules, lineOfSight, mapDoors"]
    Constants["constants/tamer-skills.ts"]
    Middleware["middleware/\ncampaign-access, dm-access\n(client route middleware)"]
    ServerAPI["server/api/**\n(40+ endpoints)"]
    ServerMiddleware["server/middleware/\n01-rate-limit.ts, encounter-sync.ts\n(global Nitro middleware)"]
    ServerUtils["server/utils/\napplyEffect, resolveNpcAttack, enemyKnockouts, triggerCounterattack, resolveSupportAttack, resolveAreaIntercedeGroup, computeAttackDamage, selectiveTargeting, encounterPayload, id, password, participantName, mapMovement, endOfTurnGravity, gridDistance, areaShapes, encounterRoom, session, campaignAuth, ownership"]
    DB["server/db/\nindex.ts + schema.ts"]
    Postgres[("PostgreSQL")]

    App --> Layouts --> Pages
    Pages --> Components
    Pages --> Composables
    Components --> Composables
    Composables --> Types
    Composables --> ServerAPI
    Components --> Types
    Components --> Data
    Pages --> Middleware
    Pages --> Utils
    Pages --> Constants
    ServerAPI --> DB
    ServerAPI --> ServerUtils
    ServerAPI --> Types
    ServerMiddleware -.intercepts.-> ServerAPI
    ServerMiddleware --> ServerUtils
    ServerUtils --> Types
    ServerUtils --> Data
    ServerUtils --> Utils
    DB --> Postgres
```

### Entry Points

| Entry | File | What It Boots |
|---|---|---|
| App shell | `app/app.vue` | Nuxt layout + page system |
| Nitro server | `.output/server/index.mjs` (built) | HTTP server, all `/api/*` routes |
| DB connection | `server/db/index.ts` | PostgreSQL client, imported by every API handler |

### Core Modules (imported by 3+ others)

`utils/naturewalk.ts` (2026-11-09l) owns element selection, passive/conditional bonuses, map terrain classification and GM settings. It is shared by movement, combat dice/defense, map previews and server damage/effect calculations. `server/utils/naturewalk.ts` resolves authoritative environment membership and the once-per-combat Water grant using bulk combat records and room positions. Existing Guiding Light refresh paths apply both systems to the same action roster; background refreshes share the record cache and remain serialized.

| Module | Imported By |
|---|---|
| `types/index.ts` | Every composable, component, API handler, server util |
| `server/db/schema.ts` | Every API handler, `server/db/index.ts`, `drizzle.config.ts` |
| `data/qualities.ts` | `QualitySelector`, `useDigimonQualities`, `useDigimonForm`, `server/api/…/actions/*` |
| `data/attacks.ts` | `AttackSelector`, `useDigimonAttacks`, `useAttackTags`, server actions |
| `data/attackConstants.ts` | `useEncounters`, `useAttackTags`, `server/utils/applyEffect`, multiple action handlers |
| `server/utils/applyEffect.ts` | `intercede-offer`, `npc-attack`, `clash-action`, `attack`, `direct`, `special-order` |
| `server/utils/computeAttackDamage.ts` | `intercede-claim.post`, `resolveNpcAttack` |
| `server/utils/clashArmor.ts` (2026-11-03c) | `computeAttackDamage.ts`, `responses.post.ts`. Itself depends on `server/utils/resolveSupportAttack.ts` (`getDigimonDerivedStats`) and `app/utils/clashRules.ts` |
| `server/utils/digizoidArmor.ts` (2026-07-28) | `computeAttackDamage.ts`, `resolveNpcAttack.ts`, `resolveSupportAttack.ts`, `responses.post.ts`, `digivolve.post.ts`, `roundStartQualityTriggers.ts` |

### Leaf Modules (imported by nothing else)

- `constants/tamer-skills.ts` — only by `TamerFormPage`

- `utils/torment-validation.ts` — only by `useTamerValidation`
- `server/utils/participantName.ts` — only by server action handlers

| Service | Protocol | Module | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| PostgreSQL | TCP (postgres driver) | `server/db/index.ts` | Only external dependency |

No HTTP clients, no third-party APIs, no webhooks.
